हरियाणा-122002 | भारत सरकार को विद्युत अधिनियम 2003 की धारा 164 के तहत बिजली के लाईनों या बिजली संयन्त्र के पारेषण के लिये या 
प्रयोजन के लिये सभी शीकतयों को प्रदान करने के लिये आवेदन करने का इरादा रखता है भारतीय टेलीग्राफ अधिनियम 1885 के तहत कार्यों के 
उचित समन्वय के लिए आवश्यक टेलीफोनिक या टेलीग्राफिक संचार, जो कि सरकार द्वारा स्थापित या बनाए गए टेलीग्राफ के उद्देश्य के लिए

उप नियम 11 के अं्तगत वर्तमान खंड 11.5 बी में, शीर्षक में दिखनेवाले शब्दों“सदस्यों” शब्दों से पहले और “का सूचीकरण”शब्दों के

है। प्रस्तावित संशोधन सूचना /सार्वजनिक टिप्पणियों / आलोचना के लिए प्रतिभूति अनुबंध (विनियमन) नियम, 1957 के नियम 18 के अनुसार 
प्रकाशित किए जाते हैं। एनसीसीएल के नियमों और उपनियमों में प्रस्तावित संशोधनों पर कोई टिप्पणी /टिप्पणियां रखने वाला कोई भी व्यक्ति इस

अभ्युक्तियों पर विचार नहीं किया जायेगा। पंद्रह दिनों की समाप्ति के तुरंत बाद मसौदे पर विचार किया जाएगा। 


ए) नेशनल कमोडिटी क्लियरिंग लिमिटेड के उपनियमों में प्रस्तावित संशोधन 


1. संक्षिप्त शीर्षक और प्रारंभ 
(1) इस संशोधन को एनसीसीएल उपनियम (संशोधन), 2023 कहा जाएगा। 
(2) यह भारत के राजपत्र में अधिसूचना की तारीख से प्रभावी होगा। 
2. उपनियम 9.1 के तहत मौजूदा उप खंड 9.1.1 के बाद एक नोट डालने का प्रस्ताव हैः 


“नोट: उपरोक्त के पूर्वाग्रह के बिना, समाशोधन निगम समय-समय पर सेबी और /या समाशोधन निगम द्वारा निर्धारित नियमों और शर्तों 
के अधीन सूचकांक वायदा और इसमे अंतर्निहित घटकों या इसके प्रकारों के बीच क्रॉस मार्जिन लाभ प्रदान कर सकता है।”

“9.1.4 प्रत्येक सदस्य अपने पास रखे गए कोलेटरल को अलग-अलग, निगरानी और रिपोर्ट करेगा, जैसा कि समय-समय पर समाशोधन 
निगम और »या सेबी द्वारा निर्धारित किया जा सकता है”।

Subject to SEBI's approval, it is proposed to make the following amendments in the Bye Laws of National Commodity & 
Derivatives Exchange Limited (NCDEX). The proposed amendments are published as per Rule 18 of Securities Contracts 
(Regulation) Rules, 1957 for information/public comments/criticism. Any person having any comments/ observations on the 
proposed amendments to the Bye Laws of NCDEX may send the same to NCDEX at gazette@ncdex.com within fifteen days 
from the date of this publication. The observation received after the said fifteenth day will not be considered. The draft will 
be taken in to consideration immediately after expiry of fifteen days, subject to approval by SEBI. 


PROPOSED AMENDMENT TO THE BYE LAWS OF THE NATIONAL COMMODITY & DERIVATIVE EXCHANGE 
LIMITED. 
1. Short title and commencement: 
a. This amendment shall be called NCDEX Bye Laws (Amendment), 2023. 
b. It shall come into force with effect from the date of notification in Gazette of India. 
2. In the existing Clause 5.2 under Bye Law 5, following amendments are notified: 
a. New Sub-clause 12 shall be inserted after existing Sub-clause 11 as under: 


“12. The Members shall adhere to all obligations with regard to periodic settlement of running account of funds of its 
clients in the manner prescribed by SEBI from time to time and as may be deemed necessary.” 


b. New Sub-clause 13 shall be inserted after newly inserted Sub-clause 12 as under: 


“13. The Exchange may prescribe norms, procedures, guidelines with regards to handling of client securities and/or 
funds by the Trading Member(s) in the manner prescribed by SEBI from time to time and as may be deemed 
necessary.”

algorithmic strategies for trading. The Exchange” shall be inserted after the word “for Algorithmic trading and” 
and before the words “shall prescribe the norms”.

“procedures, terms and conditions” and before the words “as may be directed by 85877,

Members shall only use such software as may be permitted by adhering to terms and conditions & norms and

procedures as prescribed by SEBI and/or Exchange.” shall be inserted after the words “by SEBI from time to

Subject to SEBI's approval, it is proposed to make the following amendments in the Rules & Bye Laws of National 
Commodity Clearing Limited (NCCL). The proposed amendments are published as per Rule 18 of Securities Contracts 
(Regulation) Rules, 1957 for information/public comments/criticism. Any person having any comments/ observations on the 
proposed amendments to the Rules & Bye Laws of NCCL may send the same to NCCL at compliance @nccl.co.in within 
fifteen days from the date of this publication. The observation received after the said fifteenth day will not be considered. The 
draft will be taken in to consideration immediately after expiry of fifteen days’. 


A. PROPOSED AMENDMENT TO THE BYE LAWS OF THE NATIONAL COMMODITY CLEARING LIMITED 
1. Short title and commencement 
(1) This amendment shall be called NCCL Bye Laws (Amendment), 2023. 
(2) It shall come into force with effect from the date of notification in Gazette of India. 
2. A note is inserted after the existing sub clause 9.1.1 under Bye Law 9.1: 


“Note: Without prejudice to the above, the Clearing Corporation may provide cross margin benefit 
between Index futures and its underlying constituents or its variants subject to such terms and 
conditions as maybe prescribed by SEBI and/or the Clearing Corporation from time to time.” 


3. A new sub clause as 9.1.4 is inserted after the existing sub clause 9.1.3 under Byelaw 9.1: 


“9.1.4 Every member shall segregate, monitor and report the collaterals held by it in such manner as may 
be prescribed by the Clearing Corporation and/or SEBI from time to time”. 


4. A new sub clause as 9.1.5 is inserted after the newly inserted sub clause 9.1.4 under Byelaw 9.1: 


“9.1.5 Allocation of the collaterals deposited by the Clearing member/Client, its valuation, manner of 
blocking the said collaterals towards margin requirement and withdrawal of such collateral shall be 
in a manner as prescribed by SEBI and/or Clearing Corporation from time to time”. 


5. A new sub clause as 9.1.6 is inserted after the newly inserted sub clause 9.1.5 under Byelaw 9.1: 


“9.1.6 Any false allocation by members shall be treated as a violation and disciplinary action shall be 
taken against the members” 


6. In the existing sub clause 9.7.3 under Bye Law 9.7, the following sentence in italics is inserted after the 
words “...as per directions of SEBI from time to time.” 


“The alternate tools shall be such as may be employed by the Clearing Corporation and/or as prescribed by 
SEBI under the default management process for regaining a matchbook.” 


7. A new sub clause as 10.2.4 is inserted after the existing sub clause 10.2.3 under Byelaw 10.2: 


“10.2.4 In case of a default by the Trading member or its constituents, the Clearing Member shall follow 
such procedure as may be specified by SEBI and continue to meet its obligation towards other 
constituents and the Clearing Corporation.” 


1684 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, MAY 20, 2023 (VAISAKHA 30, 1945) [PART IV 


8. A new sub clause as 10.14.2 is inserted after the existing sub clause 10.14.1 under Byelaw 10.14: 


“10.14.2 When a complaint has been lodged by a Constituent, against a Clearing Member and upon the 
failure of such Clearing Member to satisfactorily perform in its dealings or meet its obligations, 
then such complaint shall be taken up with the concerned Clearing Member and upon the said 
complaint remaining unresolved, the same shall be placed before the Grievance Redressal 
Committee or such other Committee for redressal of the grievance in accordance with such 
procedure as specified by the Clearing Corporation and/or SEBI from time to time; 


Explanation (1): 


For the purpose of this clause, the term “Constituent” shall mean Custodial Participant or a Trading 
Member as the case may be on whose instructions and/or on whose account the Clearing Member 
clears and settles the deals. 


Explanation (2): 


For the purpose of this clause, the term “Custodial Participant” shall mean a direct Constituent/client 
of a Clearing Member.” 


9. In the existing sub sub clause (e) under sub clause 13.7.1 under Bye Law 13.7, following words in italics are 
inserted along with the insertion in the Note below sub sub clause (e): 


a) New words, “higher of,” inserted after the words, “...core SGFs of other segment and,” and before the 
words, “Rs. 100 crores) ...” 


b) New words with sentence, “or the capital requirement towards orderly winding down of critical 
operations and services” inserted after the words, “Rs. 100 crores” and before the words, “equal to 
ratio of segment MRC to sum of MRCs of all segments.” 


c) New words with sentence, “or the capital requirement towards orderly winding down of critical 
operations and services, whichever is higher ” inserted in the ‘NOTE’ after the words, “SEBI has 
mandated that the said Rs.100 Crore” and before the words, “is to be excluded only when remaining 
Clearing Corporation resources...” 


10. A new sub clause as 15.2 is inserted after the existing sub clause 15.1 under Byelaw 15: 
“15.2 Disclosures by the Clearing Corporation 


The Clearing Corporation shall make such disclosures in such manner either on its own or as may be 
required by SEBI or upon the directives of any statutory, regulatory or government authorities.” 


B. PROPOSED AMENDMENT TO THE RULES OF THE NATIONAL COMMODITY CLEARING LIMITED 
1. Short title and commencement 
(1) This amendment shall be called NCCL Rules (Amendment), 2023. 
(2) It shall come into force with effect from the date of notification in Gazette of India. 
2. A new sub clause as 4.11 is inserted after existing sub clause 4.10 under Rule 4: 


“4.11 The Board, in the event of winding of critical operations and services of NCCL, shall adhere to the 
framework as prescribed by SEBI from time to time.”
